dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNative CSV / RW.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBVE5 TXT format.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMechanik DAT format.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSupported train formats:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBVE2 / BVE4, with native OpenBVE extensions.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMicrosoft Train Simulator (MSTS) - Partial support, work in progress.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOpenBVE is built in OpenGL, using the OpenTK framework for 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe original founder of this project, Michelle intended for this program to be placed in the public domain.\nIn practice over the last 10+ years I've been maintaining this, we've found that whilst public domain was a noble idea, having no recognised licence and attempting to disclaim copyright tends to produce many of it's own challenges.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAs a result, all new code is licenced under BSD-2 or a similar permissive licence (as appropriate)- Please see the source headers.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIt is our belief that BSD-2 on \u003cstrong\u003enew\u003c/strong\u003e code keeps most of original philosophical aims intact, whilst at the same time giving the benfits of a recognised licence.\nAt some point, the project will probably have to re-licence entirely, but that is somewhere in the future.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIn practical terms, what this means is that you can make any modifications to the source like and share your modifications with others.\nPlease also see the following issue for <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">OpenBVE Source Code - Readme</h2><a id="user-content-openbve-source-code---readme" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OpenBVE Source Code - Readme" href="#openbve-source-code---readme"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This repository contains the source code for the Train Simulator OpenBVE, a 3D cab based simulator.</p>
<p dir="auto">Supported route formats:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Native CSV / RW.</li>
<li>BVE5 TXT format.</li>
<li>Mechanik DAT format.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Supported train formats:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>BVE2 / BVE4, with native OpenBVE extensions.</li>
<li>Microsoft Train Simulator (MSTS) - Partial support, work in progress.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">OpenBVE is built in OpenGL, using the OpenTK framework for windowing.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Fixed Errata</h3><a id="user-content-fixed-errata" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Fixed Errata" href="#fixed-errata"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">These are described fully <a href="https://github.com/leezer3/OpenBVE/wiki/Errata">here</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Nightly Builds</h3><a id="user-content-nightly-builds" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Nightly Builds" href="#nightly-builds"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Packages</h3><a id="user-content-packages" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Packages" href="#packages"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">OpenBVE now supports the installation of 'package files' , which are intended as a replacement for the now defunct managed content. These are described fully <a href="http://openbve-project.net/packages/" rel="nofollow">here</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How to build</h3><a id="user-content-how-to-build" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How to build" href="#how-to-build"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This build has been tested to compile correctly using Visual Studio 2017 onwards and MonoDevelop. These are described fully <a href="/leezer3/OpenBVE/blob/master/Building.md">here</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">License</h3><a id="user-content-license" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: License" href="#license"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The original founder of this project, Michelle intended for this program to be placed in the public domain.
In practice over the last 10+ years I've been maintaining this, we've found that whilst public domain was a noble idea, having no recognised licence and attempting to disclaim copyright tends to produce many of it's own challenges.</p>
<p dir="auto">As a result, all new code is licenced under BSD-2 or a similar permissive licence (as appropriate)- Please see the source headers.</p>
<p dir="auto">It is our belief that BSD-2 on <strong>new</strong> code keeps most of original philosophical aims intact, whilst at the same time giving the benfits of a recognised licence.
At some point, the project will probably have to re-licence entirely, but that is somewhere in the future.</p>
<p dir="auto">In practical terms, what this means is that you can make any modifications to the source like and share your modifications with others.
Please also see the following issue for further discussion on the topic: <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="398692338" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/leezer3/OpenBVE/issues/305" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/leezer3/OpenBVE/issues/305/hovercard" href="https://github.com/leezer3/OpenBVE/issues/305">#305</a></p>
